## v2.product.init_tier_variation

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation`

This API allows you to update the tier structure of a product. Defining only color creates one tier, while color + size creates two tiers (maximum supported). Supported changes include: no tier ↔ one/two tiers, one tier ↔ two/no tier, and two tiers ↔ one/no tier. For details, see Developer Guide.  Please wait at least 5 seconds after creating an item before creating variants, as processing may be delayed.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 1000 | ID of item |
| model | object[] | ✅ |  | Model info list, model number at most 50 |
| ↳ tier_index | int32[] | ✅ | [0] | Tier index of this model.If you want to update one tier/two tier to no tier, can just pass the tier_variation and standardise_tier_variation as [], and pass the model &gt;&gt; tier_index as [], meanwhile pass the original_price, seller_stock, etc., to set the price and stock for the modified product with no tier structure. |
| ↳ original_price | float | ✅ | 38.3 | Original price of this model.For CO local VAT responsible seller：Please remember the price you set in here must be VAT inclusive. If you have any doubts on how to calculate VAT for your product please refer to the Seller Education Hub（https://seller.shopee.com.co/edu/article/13565） |
| ↳ model_sku | string |  | SKU | Seller SKU of this model, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. |
| ↳ seller_stock | object[] | ✅ |  | new stock info（Please notice that stock(including Seller Stock and Shopee Stock) should be larger than or equal to real-time reserved stock） |
| ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id, you can get the location id from v2.shop.get_warehouse_detail api, if seller don't have any warehouse, you don't need to upload this field. |
| ↳ ↳ stock | int32 | ✅ |  | stock |
| ↳ gtin_code | string |  |  | - GTIN is an identifier for trade items, developed by the international organization GS1.- They have 8 to 14 digits. The most common are UPC, EAN, JAN and ISBN.- GTIN will help boost positioning in online marketing channels like Google and Facebook.- That incorporation with GTIN will also aid in Search and Recommendation in Shopee itself allowing buyers to have higher likelihood of finding one's listing.Note: If you want to set “Item without GTIN”, please pass the gtin_code as "00".The validation rule is based on the value return in gtin_validation_rule" field in v2.product.get_item_limit API- Mandatory:&nbsp;This field is required and must contain a correctly formatted GTiN number.- Flexible: This field is required and must contain either a correctly formatted GTlN number or "00" to declare that the item/model has no valid GTlN.- Optional: This field is optional and can contain a correctly formatted GTiN number, "00" or be omitted entirely. |
| ↳ weight | float |  | 1.1 | The weight of this model, the unit is KG.If don't set the weight of this model, will use the weight of item by default.If set the dimension of this model, them must set the weight of this model. |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this model.If don't set the dimension of this model, will use the dimension of item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | Pre-order information of this model.Notes:&nbsp;If don't set the DTS of this model, will use the DTS of the item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | false | Whether the model is pre order. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  | 2 | Days to ship. Please get the days_to_ship range from the get_dts_limit API. |
| standardise_tier_variation | object[] |  |  | There is at least one standardise_tier_variation and&nbsp;tier_variation.If you want to update one tier/two tier to no tier, can just pass the tier_variation and standardise_tier_variation as [], and pass the model &gt;&gt; tier_index as [], meanwhile pass the original_price, seller_stock, etc., to set the price and stock for the modified product with no tier structure. |
| ↳ variation_id | int32 | ✅ | 1 | standardise tier variation ID. |
| ↳ variation_name | string |  | name1 | standardise tier variation name |
| ↳ variation_group_id | int32 |  | 2 | standardise tier variation group ID |
| ↳ variation_option_list | object[] | ✅ |  | standardise tier variation option list |
| ↳ ↳ variation_option_id | int32 |  |  | standardise tier variation option ID. |
| ↳ ↳ variation_option_name | string |  | option2 | standardise tier variation option value |
| ↳ ↳ image_id | string |  | 82becb4830bd2ee90ad6acf8a9dc26d7 | standardise tier variation option image ID |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_id | int64 |  | 1234 | ID of item |
| ↳ tier_variation | object[] |  |  | Variations of item |
| ↳ ↳ name | string |  | Color | Variation name |
| ↳ ↳ option_list | object[] |  |  | Options of this variation |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ image | object |  |  | Image of this option |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_url | string |  | https://cf.shopee.sg/file/82becb4830bd2ee90ad6acf8a9dc26d7 | URL of image |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ option | string |  | Red | Option name |
| ↳ model | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ tier_index | object[] |  |  | Tier index of model. Index starts from 0. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  | 3813 | ID of model |
| ↳ ↳ model_sku | string |  | SKU | Seller SKU of this model |
| ↳ ↳ price_info | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ original_price | float |  |  | Original price |
| ↳ ↳ seller_stock | object[] |  |  | new stock info |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ stock | int32 |  |  | stock |
| ↳ ↳ weight | float |  | 1.1 | The weight of this model, the unit is KG.If don't set the weight of this model, will use the weight of item by default.If set the dimension of this model, them must set the weight of this model. |
| ↳ ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this model.If don't set the dimension of this model, will use the dimension of item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 |  | 11 | The height of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 |  | 11 | The length of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 |  | 11 | The width of package for this model, the unit is CM. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/init_tier_variation?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "model": [
    {
      "gtin_code": "-",
      "model_sku": "SKU",
      "original_price": 38.3,
      "seller_stock": [
        {
          "location_id": "-",
          "stock": 0
        }
      ],
      "weight": 1.1
    }
  ],
  "standardise_tier_variation": [
    {
      "variation_group_id": 2,
      "variation_id": 1,
      "variation_name": "name1",
      "variation_option_list": [
        {
          "image_id": "82becb4830bd2ee90ad6acf8a9dc26d7",
          "variation_option_id": 0,
          "variation_option_name": "option2"
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "tier_variation": [
    {
      "name": "Color",
      "option_list": [
        {
          "image": {
            "image_id": "82becb4830bd2ee90ad6acf8a9dc26d7"
          },
          "option": "Red"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

## v2.product.update_tier_variation

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation`

This api can only be used without changing the tier structure, you can add options, delete options, and update the option image by this api. More detail please check: https://open.shopee.com/developer-guide/219

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 1000 | ID of item. |
| model_list | object[] |  |  | Item's model list |
| ↳ model_id | int64 | ✅ | 1002066586922 | ID of model |
| ↳ tier_index | int64[] | ✅ | [1,1] | Model's tier_variation |
| standardise_tier_variation | object[] |  |  | &nbsp;item standardise tier variation&nbsp;There is at least one standardise_tier_variation and&nbsp;tier_variation&nbsp; |
| ↳ variation_id | int64 | ✅ |  | standardise tier variation ID |
| ↳ variation_name | string |  |  | standardise tier variation name |
| ↳ variation_group_id | int64 |  |  | standardise tier variation group id |
| ↳ variation_option_list | object[] | ✅ |  | standardise tier variation option list |
| ↳ ↳ variation_option_id | int64 | ✅ |  | standardise tier variation option ID |
| ↳ ↳ variation_option_name | string |  |  | standardise tier variation option value |
| ↳ ↳ image_id | string |  |  | ID of image |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.uat.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_tier_variation?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "item_id": 1000,
  "tier_variation": [
    {
      "name": "-",
      "option_list": [
        {
          "image": {
            "image_id": "-"
          },
          "option": "-"
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 178312 | The ID of the item |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Product Management
- Accounting And Finance
- Customer Service
- Customized APP
- Ads Service
- Swam ERP
- Livestream Management
- Ads Facil
- Affiliate Marketing Solution Management
- Shopee Video Management

## v2.product.add_model

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/add_model`

Add model. More detail please check: https://open.shopee.com/developer-guide/219

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_model` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/add_model` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/add_model` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_model` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 1000 | ID of item |
| model_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Model list |
| ↳ tier_index | int32[] | ✅ | [0] | Tier index of model |
| ↳ original_price | float | ✅ | 38.3 | Normal stock for price |
| ↳ model_sku | string |  | SKU | Seller sku, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. |
| ↳ seller_stock | object[] | ✅ |  | new stock info for model（Please notice that stock(including Seller Stock and Shopee Stock) should be larger than or equal to real-time reserved stock） |
| ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id,&nbsp;you can get the location id from v2.shop.get_warehouse_detail api, if seller don't have any warehouse, you don't need to upload this field. |
| ↳ ↳ stock | int32 | ✅ |  | stock |
| ↳ gtin_code | string |  |  | - GTIN is an identifier for trade items, developed by the international organization GS1.- They have 8 to 14 digits. The most common are UPC, EAN, JAN and ISBN.- GTIN will help boost positioning in online marketing channels like Google and Facebook.- That incorporation with GTIN will also aid in Search and Recommendation in Shopee itself allowing buyers to have higher likelihood of finding one's listing.Note: If you want to set “Item without GTIN”, please pass the gtin_code as "00".The validation rule is based on the value return in gtin_validation_rule" field in v2.product.get_item_limit API- Mandatory:&nbsp;This field is required and must contain a correctly formatted GTiN number.- Flexible: This field is required and must contain either a correctly formatted GTlN number or "00" to declare that the item/model has no valid GTlN.- Optional: This field is optional and can contain a correctly formatted GTiN number, "00" or be omitted entirely. |
| ↳ weight | float |  | 1.1 | The weight of this model, the unit is KG.If don't set the weight of this model, will use the weight of item by default.If set the dimension of this model, them must set the weight of this model. |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this model.If don't set the dimension of this model, will use the dimension of item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | Pre-order information of this model.Notes:&nbsp;If don't set the DTS of this model, will use the DTS of the item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | false | Whether the model is pre order. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  | 2 | Days to ship. Please get the days_to_ship range from the get_dts_limit API. |

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

If you have any doubts on how to calculate VAT for your product please refer to the Seller Education Hub（https://seller.shopee.com.co/edu/article/13565） |

### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_model?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "model_list": [
    {
      "gtin_code": "-",
      "model_sku": "SKU",
      "original_price": 38.3,
      "seller_stock": [
        {
          "location_id": "-",
          "stock": 0
        }
      ],
      "weight": 1.1
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
					"location_id": "-",
					"stock": 0
				}
			],
			"gtin_code": "-",
			"weight": 1.1
		}
	]
}
```

```json
						"original_price": 52.4
					}
				],
				"seller_stock": [
					{
						"location_id": "-",

}
```

| `error_holiday_on_add_item` | Shop is under vocation mode. |
| `error_nil_name_new_item_model` | Item tier_variation name cannot be empty. |
| `error_price_exceed_min_limitt` | Original_price is less than min price limit. |
| `error_price_exceed_max_limitt` | Original_price is bigger than max price limit. |
| `error_wholesale_price_less_than_ratio_limit` | Wholesale price is less than ratio limit. |
| `error_param` | Can not update item with different stock structure. Can only update seller stock with location id when existing seller stock have location id. Can only update seller stock without location id when existing seller stock without location id. |
| `error_server` | Interal error, please contact openapi team. |
| `error_slash_price_nomodel_to_models_lock` | In slash sale, can't add model. |
| `error_price_should_be_same_for_wholesales` | All model price should be the same when the item has been set wholesales. |

## v2.product.update_model

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/update_model`

Update seller sku/ pre order/ model status for model.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_model` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/update_model` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/update_model` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_model` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 1000 | ID of item |
| model | object[] | ✅ |  | Length should be between 1 to 50 |
| ↳ model_id | int64 | ✅ | 1625698439 | ID of model |
| ↳ model_sku | string | ✅ | abc | Seller SKU for model, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. CNSC and KRSC sellers are not allowed to update the MPSKU model sku. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | false | Pre-order |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 | ✅ | 3 | The days to ship. Only work for is_pre_order=true |
| ↳ gtin_code | string |  |  | - GTIN is an identifier for trade items, developed by the international organization GS1.- They have 8 to 14 digits. The most common are UPC, EAN, JAN and ISBN.- GTIN will help boost positioning in online marketing channels like Google and Facebook.- That incorporation with GTIN will also aid in Search and Recommendation in Shopee itself allowing buyers to have higher likelihood of finding one's listing.Note: If you want to set “Item without GTIN”, please pass the gtin_code as "00".The validation rule is based on the value return in gtin_validation_rule" field in v2.product.get_item_limit API- Mandatory:&nbsp;This field is required and must contain a correctly formatted GTiN number.- Flexible: This field is required and must contain either a correctly formatted GTlN number or "00" to declare that the item/model has no valid GTlN.- Optional: This field is optional and can contain a correctly formatted GTiN number, "00" or be omitted entirely. |
| ↳ model_status | string |  |  | can be&nbsp;"NORMAL" or "UNAVAILABLE". Only CNSC and KRSC sellers can set the model_status.&nbsp;Normal models can be sold on the buyer's side, and UNAVAILABLE models cannot be sold on the buyer's side. |
| ↳ weight | float |  | 1.1 | The weight of this model, the unit is KG.If don't set the weight of this model, will use the weight of item by default.If set the dimension of this model, them must set the weight of this model. |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this model.If don't set the dimension of this model, will use the dimension of item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this model, the unit is CM. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_model?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "model": [
    {
      "model_sku": "abc",
      "pre_order": {
        "is_pre_order": False
      },
      "weight": 1.1
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"model": [
		{
			"model_sku": "abc",
			"pre_order": {
				"is_pre_order": false
			},
			"weight": 1.1
		}
	]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
"error": "",
"message": "",
"warning": "",
"request_id": "aaaaaaa",
}
```

### Error Example

```json
    "error": "product.error_param",
    "message": "invalid field ItemId: value must  Not Null",
    "request_id": "d907a5c84ce33078ab561912bf7a22f5"
}
```

## v2.product.update_stock

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/update_stock`

Use this API to update one item_id for each call, but still can support updating multiple model_ids stock of the same item_id (If you need batch modification, please call multiple times)This API will update only "seller_stock".Whenever there is a promotion ongoing or upcoming, the total stock must be larger than or equal to real-time “reserved_stock” promotion stock (Please check v2.get_item_promotion API for more details). Items that are deleted will not be allowed to modify stock.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_stock` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/update_stock` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/update_stock` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_stock` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 1000 | ID of item. |
| stock_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Length should be between 1 to 50. |
| ↳ model_id | int64 |  |  | 0 for no model item. |
| ↳ seller_stock | object[] | ✅ |  | new stock info（Please notice that stock(including Seller Stock and Shopee Stock) should be larger than or equal to real-time reserved stock） |
| ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id, you can get the location id from v2.shop.get_warehouse_detail api, if seller don't have any warehouse, you don't need to upload this field. |
| ↳ ↳ stock | int64 | ✅ |  | stock |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ failure_list | object[] |  |  | Fail model list. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  |  | ID of model. |
| ↳ ↳ failed_reason | string |  |  | Reason for failure. |
| ↳ success_list | object[] |  |  | Success model list. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  |  | ID of model. |
| ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id; This field and the stock field are returned in pairs |
| ↳ ↳ stock | int64 |  |  | stock;This field is returned if seller stock is used in the request, and normal stock fields are not returned. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_stock?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "item_id": 1000,
  "stock_list": [
    {
      "model_id": 0,

      "seller_stock": [
        {
          "location_id": "-",
          "stock": 0
        }
      ]
    }
  ]
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"item_id": 1000,
	"stock_list": [
		{
			"model_id": 0,
		
			"seller_stock": [
				{
					"location_id": "-",
					"stock": 0
				}
			]
		}
	]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
	"error": "-",
	"message": "-",
	"warning": "-",
	"request_id": "-",
	"response": {
		"failure_list": [
			{
				"model_id": 0,
				"failed_reason": "-"
			}
		],
		"success_list": [
			{
				"model_id": 0,
				"location_id": "-",
				"stock": 0
			}
		]
	}
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "product.error_param",
    "message": "invalid field ItemId: value must  Not Null",
    "request_id": "11484dd6d9e203edd5d332551249de57"
}
```

### Endpoints

| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |

| ↳ ↳ ↳ start_time | timestamp |  |  | Promotion start tiem. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ end_time | timestamp |  |  | Promotion end item. |

| ↳ ↳ item_id | int64 |  |  | The identity of item. |
| ↳ ↳ failed_reason | string |  |  | Fail reason. |

## v2.product.search_item

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/search_item`

Use this call to search item.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/search_item` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/search_item` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/search_item` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/search_item` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| offset | string |  | 0 | Specifies the starting entry of data to return in the current call. Default is empty. if data is more than one page, the offset can be some entry to start next call. |
| page_size | int | ✅ | 10 | the size of one page. |
| item_name | string |  | apple | name of item. |
| attribute_status | int |  | 2 | 1:get item lack of requires attribute.   2:get item lack of optional attribute. |
| item_sku | string |  | sku | sku. If you search for item_sku and item_name at the same time, only the results that match item_sku will be returned. If you search for item_sku and attribute_status at the same time, the results that match both item_sku and attribute_status will be returned. |
| item_status | string[] |  | ["NORMAL"] | NORMAL/BANNED/UNLIST/REVIEWING/SELLER_DELETE/SHOPEE_DELETEIf you want to search multiple status, please upload the url like this: item_status=NORMAL&amp;item_status=BANNED |
| deboost_only | boolean |  | true | If deboost_only is true, then API will return items whose deboost is true, if deboost_only is empty or false, then API will return items whose deboost is true and false simultaneously |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  |  Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  |  Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  |  Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_id_list | int[] |  |  | List of  item ID. |
| ↳ total_count | int |  |  | Total num of items match condation. |
| ↳ next_offset | string |  |  | If has_next_page is true, this value need set to next request.offset |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/search_item?access_token=access_token&attribute_status=2&item_name=apple&offset=0&page_size=10&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 |  | 16509872 | The identity of product item. |
| comment_id | int64 |  | 120590834 | The identity of comment. |
| cursor | string | ✅ |  | Specifies the starting entry of data to return in the current call. Default is "". If data is more than one page, the offset can be some entry to start next call. |
| page_size | int32 | ✅ | 10 | Each result set is returned as a page of entries. Use the "page_size" filters to control the maximum number of entries to retrieve per page (i.e., per call). This integer value is used to specify the maximum number of entries to return in a single "page" of data. The limit of page_size if between 1 and 100. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3e0976b06baa4e059b278ef1fdae1f01 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | common.error_auth | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | Invalid access_token. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ more | boolean |  | false | This is to indicate whether the comment list is more than one page. If this value is true, you may want to continue to check next page to retrieve the rest of comments. But only respond 500 comments at most through OpenAPI, if there are more than 500, this field "more" also respond "true". |
| ↳ item_comment_list | object[] |  |  | The comment data list of the items. |
| ↳ ↳ order_sn | string |  | 200922CTK2J6KN | Shopee's unique identifier for an order. |
| ↳ ↳ comment_id | string |  | 120590834 | The identity of comment. |
| ↳ ↳ comment | string |  | thank you | The content of the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ buyer_username | string |  | tom | The username of the buyer who posted the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ item_id | int64 |  | 16509872 | The commented item's id |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  | 0 | Shopee's unique identifier for a model of an item. It will only return 0 now.&nbsp;Will be offline on 2024-12-27, please switch to use model_id_list. |
| ↳ ↳ rating_star | int32 |  | 5 | Buyer's rating for the item. |
| ↳ ↳ editable | string |  | EDITABLE | The editable status of the comment. The value may be one of  EXPIRED/EDITABLE/HAVE_EDIT_ONCE. |
| ↳ ↳ hidden | boolean |  | false | The comment is hidden or not. |
| ↳ ↳ create_time | timestamp |  | 1603153057 | The create time of the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ comment_reply | object |  |  | The reply of the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ reply | string |  | Thank you for your support. Look forward to your next visit. | The content of reply. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ hidden | boolean |  | false | The comment reply is hidden or not. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ create_time | timestamp |  | 1712053073 | The time the seller replied to the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id_list | int64[] |  |  | List of model id of the buyer's purchase corresponding to the comment.&nbsp; |
| ↳ ↳ media | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ image_url_list | string[] |  |  | List of image url uploaded by the buyer in the comment. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ video_url_list | string[] |  |  | List of video url uploaded by the buyer in the comment. |
| ↳ next_cursor | string |  | "20" | If more is true, you should pass the next_cursor in the next request as cursor. The value of next_cursor will be empty string when more is false. |

## v2.product.register_brand

**Method:** `POST`

Use this call to register a brand.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/register_brand` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/register_brand` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/register_brand` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/register_brand` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| original_brand_name | string | ✅ | Nike | Brand name, length<=254. |
| category_list | int[] | ✅ | [16521,16522] | Category_id list for this brand, please input category in L1 or L2. Max input num of category_id is 50. |
| product_image | object | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ image_id_list | string[] | ✅ |  | Image Id of product image for this brand, max input num of file = 10 ,each file's length<=498. ID market is optional.	 |
| app_logo_image_id | string |  | 6373157f9408b42c8aacda1d63d3a209 | Image_id  of logo for  app client,please input hashcode of this picture. |
| brand_website | string |  | www.nike.com | Official website of brand, length<=254. |
| brand_description | string |  | Our mission is what drives us to do everything possible to expand human potential. We do that by creating groundbreaking sport innovations, by making our products more sustainably, by building a creative and diverse global team and by making a positive impact in communities where we live and work | The description of this brand, can input the information, length<=254. |
| additional_information | string |  | additional notes or comment can seller can add | Additional notes or comment can seller can add, length<=254. |
| pc_logo_image_id | string |  | 6373157f9408b42c8aacda1d63d3a209 | Image_id  of logo for  pc client,please input hashcode of this picture. |
| brand_region | string | ✅ | US | origin region of brand. |
| licenses | object[] |  |  | For appeal blacklisted brand data |
| ↳ file_name | string |  | image.jpg | Brand registration certificate image name, len &lt;&nbsp;254 |
| ↳ file_hash | string |  | 6373157f9408b42c8aacda1d63d3a209 | Image id of brand registration certificate image , max input num of file = 1 , each file's length&lt;=498 |
| brand_registration_website | string |  | https://www.jumbomark.com/indonesia/trademark-registration/mischief-DID2019017755 | The link to brand registration website, It is mandatory when brand name hit blacklist.len&lt;254 |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| request_id | string |  | 3e0976b06baa4e059b278ef1fdae1f01 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| error | string |  | product.duplicate_request | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | You has replied this comment already. | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ brand_id | int |  |  | The identity of brand. |
| ↳ original_brand_name | string |  |  | Brand name |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/register_brand?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "additional_information": "additional notes or comment can seller can add",
  "app_logo_image_id": "6373157f9408b42c8aacda1d63d3a209",

We do that by creating groundbreaking sport innovations, by making our products more sustainably, by building a creative and diverse global team and by making a positive impact in communities where we live and work",
  "brand_website": "www.nike.com",
  "category_list": [
    16521,
    16522
  ],
  "original_brand_name": "Nike",
  "pc_logo_image_id": "6373157f9408b42c8aacda1d63d3a209",
  "product_image": {
    "image_id_list": [
      "-"
    ]
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)
```

| `error_sign` | Wrong sign. |

| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |

| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |
| `error_param_shop_id_not_found` | Shop_id is not found. |
| `error_param` | brand image is required |
| `error_invalid_category` | Invalid category ID {{.error_info}} |
| `unsupport_region_for_register_brand` | The market this shop belongs to do not support brand registration. |
| `error_busi_duplicated` | Brand already exists. If you want to add available category for this brand, you can change the category. |
| `error_busi_pending_qc` | The brand you registered is in the inspection process. Please contact Shopee Customer Service for more information. |
| `error_busi_blacklist	` | This brand name can't be used in several selected categories. You can contact Shopee CS for brand appeal offline if need. |
| `error_busi_blacklist_need_license` | Risky brand name, please provide related license and brand registration website to register this specific brand. |

- Seller In House System

## v2.product.get_recommend_attribute

**Method:** `GET`

Get recommend attributes.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |

| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |

| cover_image_id | int |  |  | Cover image id of item |
| category_id | int | ✅ | 14695 | ID of category |

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  | 98eae35efff24dd0974c21a847127184	 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |

| ↳ ↳ attribute_id | int |  |  | ID of attribute. |
| ↳ ↳ attribute_value_list | object[] |  |  | Value list of this attribute. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ value_id | int |  |  | ID of attribute value. |

## v2.product.get_weight_recommendation

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation`

Get recommended weight. Now only BR shop support to use this api to get recommended weight.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_name | string | ✅ | paper | Name of the item in local language. |
| cover_image_id | string | ✅ | e9a76cf159c3e7f12510a7017e120467 | Image id of first product image.&nbsp; |
| category_id | int | ✅ | 100061 | Shopee's unique identifier for a category. |
| attribute_list | object[] | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ attribute_id | int | ✅ | 100019 | The Identify of each attribute. |
| ↳ attribute_value_list | object[] | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ value_id | int | ✅ | 1009 | Unique identifier for value of this item attribute. |
| ↳ ↳ original_value_name | string |  | value | Value name of this item attribute. |
| ↳ ↳ value_unit | string |  | kg | Value unit of this item attribute. |
| brand_id | int | ✅ | 100021 | Id of brand. |
| description_type | string | ✅ | extended | Type of description, values: See Data Definition- description_type (normal , extended). |
| description | string |  |  | If description_type is normal , Description information should be set by this field. |
| description_info | object |  |  | New description field. Only whitelist sellers can use it. If you use the field, please upload the description_type=extended. |
| ↳ extended_description | object |  |  | If description_type is extended , Description information should be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ field_list | object[] |  |  | Field of extended description. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ field_type | string |  | text | Type of extended description field, values: See Data Definition- description_field_type (text , image). |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ text | string |  | desc text | If field_type is text, text information will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ image_info | object |  |  | If field_type is image, image information will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_id | string |  | 564d29618a333337fa1798f20476c4f5 | Image id. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  | 41b0c8c1c414ed0624bf1c9ebdb21d20 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ normal_weight_range | float[] |  | [0.1,0.5] | Recommended weight range, in kg. If there are no recommended results, return empty. |
| error | string |  | error_param | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://open.admin.uat.shopee.io/api/v2/product/get_weight_recommendation?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "attribute_list": [
    {
      "attribute_id": 0,
      "attribute_value_list": [
        {
          "original_value_name": "-",
          "value_id": 0,
          "value_unit": "-"
        }
      ]
    }
  ],
  "brand_id": 0,
  "category_id": 0,
  "cover_image_id": "xaszciahddms",
  "description": "-",
  "description_info": {
    "extended_description": {
      "field_list": [
        {
          "field_type": "-",
          "image_info": {
            "image_id": "-"
          },
          "text": "-"
        }
      ]
    }
  },
  "description_type": "-",
  "item_name": "paper"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.request("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

### Request Payload

```json
{
	"item_name": "paper",
	"cover_image_id": "e9a76cf159c3e7f12510a7017e120467",
	"category_id": 100061,
	"attribute_list": [
		{
			"attribute_id": 100019,
			"attribute_value_list": [
				{
					"value_id": 0,
					"original_value_name": "value",
					"value_unit": "kg"
				}
			]
		}
	],
	"brand_id": 100021,
	"description_type": "extended",
	"description_info": {
		"extended_description": {
			"field_list": [
				{
					"field_type": "text",
					"text": "desc text",
				}
			]
		}
	}
}
```

```json
	"message": "",
	"warning": "",
	"request_id": "41b0c8c1c414ed0624bf1c9ebdb21d20",

		]
```

| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |

| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_size_chart_detail` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| size_chart_id | int | ✅ | 700024639 | ID of new size chart |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ size_chart_id | int |  | 700024639 | ID of new size chart |
| ↳ size_chart_name | string |  | T shirt | name of new size chart |
| ↳ size_chart_table | object |  |  | new size chart is a table format which include multiple columns. each column has column header (measurement) and multiple values (measurement value) of this column. |
| ↳ ↳ column_list | object[] |  |  | column list of new size chart table. it include one column (measurement) and multiple values (measurement value) |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ measurement | object |  |  | this is the column header which means a kind of measurement |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ input_type | string |  | Input Range Number | there are 3 kinds of measurement type: Single Dropdown, Input Single Number, Input Range Number. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ display_name | string |  | weight | name of column header (measurement) |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ unit | string |  | kg | the unit of this size measurement. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ measurement_value_list | object[] |  |  | the list of measurement value |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ value | float |  | 50.5 | if the input_type of measurement is single input number, measurement will have one value which is returned by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ min_value | float |  | 52.5 | if the input_type of measurement is input range number, measurement will be a range which is returned by 2 fields: min_value and max_value. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ max_value | float |  | 52.5 | if the input_type of measurement is input range number, measurement will be a range which is returned by 2 fields: min_value and max_value. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ option | string |  | M | if the input_type of measurement is single dropdown, measurement will have one value which is returned by this field. |

```python

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.request("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

## v2.product.get_item_violation_info

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_item_violation_info`

get item violation info

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_item_violation_info` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/get_item_violation_info` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/get_item_violation_info` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_item_violation_info` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id_list | int[] | ✅ | [34001,34002] | item_id list; limit [0,50] |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ item_id | int |  | 3400133011 | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |
| ↳ ↳ item_name | string |  | test | Name of the item. |
| ↳ ↳ item_status | string |  | BANNED | Enumerated type that defines the current status of the item. Applicable values: NORMAL, BANNED, UNLIST, SELLER_DELETE, SHOPEE_DELETE, REVIEWING. |
| ↳ ↳ deboost | boolean |  | true | If deboost is true, means that the item's search ranking is lowered. |
| ↳ ↳ item_status_details | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ violation_type | string |  | Prohibited Listing | Violation types defined by Shopee.&nbsp;Applicable values:&nbsp;Prohibited ListingCounterfeit and IP InfringementSpamInappropriate ImageInsufficient InformationMall Listing ImprovementOther Listing Improvement |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ violation_reason | string |  | License Reason | The reason for violation. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ suggestion | string |  | Upload license | Shopee provides you with suggestions for modifying items. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ fix_deadline_time | timestamp |  | 1705227588 | Action required deadline. Empty if no deadline. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ update_time | timestamp |  | 1705054788 | Latest update time. |
| ↳ ↳ deboost_details | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ violation_type | string |  | Prohibited Listing | Violation types defined by Shopee.&nbsp;Applicable values:&nbsp;Prohibited ListingCounterfeit and IP InfringementSpamInappropriate ImageInsufficient InformationMall Listing ImprovementOther Listing Improvement |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ violation_reason | string |  | Wrong Category | The reason for violation. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ suggestion | string |  | The item is in wrong category, please update to the suggested_category | Shopee provides you with suggestions for modifying items. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ suggested_category | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ category_id | int |  | 107478 | ID for Shopee suggested category. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ category_name | string |  | Personal Care | Default name for Shopee suggested category. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ fix_deadline_time | timestamp |  | 1705202227 | Action required deadline. Empty if no deadline. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ update_time | timestamp |  | 1704943027 | Latest update time. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if one element hit error. |
| ↳ ↳ fail_message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if one element hit error. |

## v2.product.get_variations

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_variation_tree`

Get the standardized tier variation defined by Shopee, which is currently a three-layer tree structure. 
The top layer is variations, the second layer is groups, groups are used to divide options, and the third layer is options.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_variation_tree` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/get_variation_tree` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/get_variation_tree` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_variation_tree` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| category_id | int | ✅ |  | Leaf category id |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| data | object |  |  | standardized tier variation data |
| ↳ standardise_variation_list | object[] |  |  | standardized tier variation tree |
| ↳ ↳ variation_id | int |  | 123 |  |
| ↳ ↳ variation_name | string |  | Color |  |
| ↳ ↳ variation_group_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ variation_group_id | int |  | 1234 |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ variation_group_name | string |  | group1 |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ variation_option_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ variation_option_id | int |  | 12345 |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ variation_option_name | string |  | yellow |  |

### Endpoints

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |

| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |

| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |

### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ success_item_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ item_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |
| ↳ ↳ quality_level | int32 |  |  | Item's latest content quality level. Applicable values:0: NONE (No quality level for item in SELLER_DELETE / SHOPEE_DELETE / BANNED status)1: TO_BE_IMPROVED2: QUALIFIED3: EXCELLENT |
| ↳ ↳ unfinished_task | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ issue_type | int32 |  |  | Item's content issue. Applicable values:1: TOO_FEW_IMAGES  2: WRONG_CATEGORY  3: TOO_FEW_ATTRIBUTES_FOR_QUALIFIED4: LACK_OF_SIZE_CHART  5: LACK_OF_STANDARD_VARIATION 6: LACK_BRAND  7: TOO_SHORT_DESCRIPTION  8: TOO_SHORT_OR_TOO_LONG_NAME9: WRONG_WEIGHT  10: LACK_OF_VIDEO11: TOO_FEW_ATTRIBUTES_FOR_EXCELLENT |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ suggestion | string |  |  | System&nbsp;suggestion for item's content issue. Applicable values:Add at least 3 imagesAdopt suggested categoryAdd at least 1 attributesAdd size chartAdopt the color or size variationAdd brand infoAdd at least 100 characters or 1 image for descAdd characters for name to 25~100Adopt suggested weightAdd videoAdd at least 3 attributes |
| ↳ failure_item_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ item_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |
| ↳ ↳ failed_reason | string |  |  | Item's failure reason. |

| ↳ ↳ ↳ issue_type | int32 |  |  | Item's content issue. Applicable values:1: TOO_FEW_IMAGES2: WRONG_CATEGORY3: TOO_FEW_ATTRIBUTES_FOR_QUALIFIED4: LACK_OF_SIZE_CHART5: LACK_OF_STANDARD_VARIATION6: LACK_BRAND7: TOO_SHORT_DESCRIPTION8: TOO_SHORT_OR_TOO_LONG_NAME9: WRONG_WEIGHT10: LACK_OF_VIDEO11: TOO_FEW_ATTRIBUTES_FOR_EXCELLENT |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ suggestion | string |  |  | System&nbsp;suggestion for item's content issue. Applicable values:Add at least 3 imagesAdopt suggested categoryAdd at least 1 attributesAdd size chartAdopt the color or size variationAdd brand infoAdd at least 100 characters or 1 image for descAdd characters for name to 25~100Adopt suggested weightAdd videoAdd at least 3 attributes |

## v2.product.get_kit_item_limit

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_limit`

Get the limit of Kit item.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_limit` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_limit` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_limit` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_limit` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| category_id | int64 |  | 400055 | Shopee's unique identifier for a category. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  | success | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Warning message. |
| request_id | string |  | 28ad0434c6954963a2a6d6ed7195554d | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ price_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | float |  | 5.5 | Item price max limit. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | float |  | 10000000.0 | Item price min limit. |
| ↳ item_name_length_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | int64 |  | 5 | Item name length min limit. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | int64 |  | 99 | Item name length max limit. |
| ↳ item_image_count_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | int64 |  | 1 | Item image count min limit. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | int64 |  | 10 | Item image count max limit. |
| ↳ description_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ description_length_min | int64 |  | 10 | Item description length min limit. |
| ↳ ↳ description_length_max | int64 |  | 499 | length max limit for item extended description text part. |
| ↳ ↳ description_text_length_min | int64 |  | 10 | length min limit for item extended description text part, when one of the minimum limits for image and text is reached, the item can be added or updated successfully. |
| ↳ ↳ description_text_length_max | int64 |  | 499 | length max limit for item extended description text part  |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_num_min | int64 |  | 1 | length min limit for item extended description image num, when one of the minimum limits for image and text is reached, the item can be added or updated successfully. |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_num_max | int64 |  | 5 | length max limit for item extended description image num. |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_width_min | int64 |  | 1 | length min limit for item extended description image width. |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_height_min | int64 |  | 1 | length min limit for item extended description image hight. |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_aspect_ratio_min | float |  | 0.5 | length min limit for item extended description image aspect ( aspect_ratio= image width / image hight ). |
| ↳ ↳ description_image_aspect_ratio_max | float |  | 32.0 | length max limit for item extended description image aspect ( aspect_ratio= image width / image hight ). |
| ↳ tier_variation_name_length_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | int32 |  | 1 | Item tier variation name length min limit. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | int32 |  | 50 | Item tier variation name length max limit. |
| ↳ tier_variation_option_length_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | int32 |  | 1 | Item tier variation option length min limit. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | int32 |  | 50 | Item tier variation option length max limit. |
| ↳ weight_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ weight_mandatory | boolean |  | true | Whether weight is mandatory or not for the category. |
| ↳ dimension_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ dimension_mandatory | boolean |  | true | Whether dimension is mandatory or not for the category. |
| ↳ dts_limit | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ non_pre_order_days_to_ship | int32 |  | 2 | Days to ship for non pre-order products. |
| ↳ ↳ support_pre_order | boolean |  | true | Whether support pre_order for the category. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship_limit | object |  |  | Days to ship for pre-order products. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ min_limit | int32 |  | 3 | Min limit of days to ship for pre-order products. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ max_limit | int32 |  | 30 | Max limit of days to ship for pre-order products. |
| ↳ component_count_limit_of_single_model | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ min_limit | int32 |  | 2 | Item count min limit that&nbsp;each kit variations support. |
| ↳ ↳ max_limit | int32 |  | 10 | Item count max limit that&nbsp;each kit variations support. |

```python
payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.RPCRequest("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)
```
