## v2.product.add_kit_item

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/add_kit_item`

Create the kit item by selecting multiple items and setting main component and quantity per kit.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_kit_item` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/add_kit_item` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/add_kit_item` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_kit_item` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_setting | object | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ item_name | string | ✅ | item name sample | The name of this kit item. |
| ↳ images | object | ✅ |  | Item images with 1:1 ratio. |
| ↳ ↳ image_id_list | string[] | ✅ |  | ID of image. |
| ↳ long_images | object |  |  | Item images with 3:4 ratio. |
| ↳ ↳ image_id_list | string[] | ✅ |  | ID of image. |
| ↳ video_upload_id | string[] |  | ["sg_f4bde9bc-ff3c-485e-a6dd-3161dab4b942_000000"] | Video upload ID returned from video uploading API. Only accept one video_upload_id. |
| ↳ description | string |  | item description sample | If description_type is normal, description information should be set by this field. |
| ↳ description_info | object |  |  | Rich text description field. Only whitelist sellers can use it. If you use the field, please upload the description_type=extended otherwise api will return error. If you don't use this field, you don't need to upload the description_type or upload description_type=normal |
| ↳ ↳ extended_description | object | ✅ |  | If description_type is extended , Description information should be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ field_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Field of extended description. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ field_type | string | ✅ |  | Type of extended description field. See Data Definition- description_field_type (text , image). |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ text | string | ✅ |  | If field_type is text, text information will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_info | object |  |  | If field_type is image, image will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_id | string | ✅ |  | Image id. |
| ↳ description_type | string | ✅ | extended | See Data Definition- description_type (normal , extended). If you want to use extended_description, this field must be inputed. |
| ↳ logistic_info | object[] | ✅ |  | Logistic channel setting. |
| ↳ ↳ logistic_id | int64 | ✅ |  | ID of the channel. |
| ↳ ↳ enabled | boolean | ✅ |  | Whether channel is enabled for this kit item. |
| ↳ ↳ shipping_fee | float |  |  | Shipping fee. Only needed when logistics fee_type = CUSTOM_PRICE. |
| ↳ ↳ size_id | int64 |  |  | Size ID. Only needed when logistic fee_type = SIZE_SELECTION. |
| ↳ ↳ is_free | boolean |  |  | Whether cover shipping fee for buyer. |
| ↳ unlisted | boolean |  | false | Unlist or not. |
| ↳ item_sku | string |  | item sku sample | SKU tag of item |
| ↳ weight | float | ✅ | 1.1 | The weight of this kit item, the unit is KG. |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this kit item. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | Pre order setting. |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | true | Whether kit item is pre order. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  | 10 | The guaranteed days to ship orders. Please get the days_to_ship range from get_kit_item_limit api. |
| ↳ model_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Model info list, model number at most 9. |
| ↳ ↳ tier_index | int32[] | ✅ | [0] | Tier index of this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ model_sku | string |  | model sku sample | Seller SKU of this kit model, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. |
| ↳ ↳ original_price | float | ✅ | 38.3 | Original price of this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ component_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Please get the amount of item/model that one kit model support from get_kit_item_limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ component_item_id | int64 | ✅ | 892568916 | ID of the item that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ component_model_id | int64 |  | 8501462426 | ID of the model that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ quantity | int32 | ✅ | 2 | The amount of the item/model that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ main_component | boolean |  | true | Whether this item/model is the main component for this kit.One kit item can only have one item/model as main component. |
| ↳ tier_variation_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Tier variation info list.&nbsp;Only support one tier variation, and each kit item can have from 1 to 9 kit variations. |
| ↳ ↳ name | string |  |  | Tier variation name. |
| ↳ ↳ option_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Tier variation option info list. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ option | string | ✅ |  | Option name. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ image | object |  |  | Option image. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_id | string | ✅ |  | ID of image. If you choose to define, you need to define an image for all options. |
| sync_setting | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ auto_sync_dts | boolean | ✅ | true | Auto sync the pre_order setting from main component or not. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  |  |
| message | string |  |  |  |
| warning | string |  |  |  |
| request_id | string |  |  |  |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_id | int64 |  |  |  |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/add_kit_item?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "item_setting": {
    "description": "item description sample",
    "description_info": {
      "extended_description": {
        "field_list": [
          {
            "field_type": "-",
            "image_info": {
              "image_id": "-"
            },
            "text": "-"
          }
        ]
      }
    },
    "description_type": "extended",
    "dimension": {
      "package_height": 11,
      "package_length": 11,
      "package_width": 11
    },
    "images": {
      "image_id_list": [
        "-"
      ]
    },
    "item_name": "item name sample",
    "item_sku": "item sku sample",
    "logistic_info": [
      {
        "enabled": True,
        "is_free": True,
        "logistic_id": 0,
        "shipping_fee": 0.1,
        "size_id": 0
      }
    ],
    "long_images": {
      "image_id_list": [
        "-"
      ]
    },
    "model_list": [
      {
        "component_list": [
          {
            "component_item_id": 892568916,
            "component_model_id": 8501462426,
            "main_component": True,
            "quantity": 2
          }
        ],
        "model_sku": "model sku sample",
        "original_price": 38.3,
        "tier_index": [
          0
        ]
      }
    ],
    "pre_order": {
      "days_to_ship": 10,
      "is_pre_order": True
    },
    "tier_variation_list": [
      {
        "name": "-",
        "option_list": [
          {
            "image": {
              "image_id": "-"
            },
            "option": "-"
          }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "unlisted": False,
    "video_upload_id": [
      "sg_f4bde9bc-ff3c-485e-a6dd-3161dab4b942_000000"
    ],
    "weight": 1.1
  },
  "sync_setting": {
    "auto_sync_dts": True
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)
```

## v2.product.update_kit_item

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/update_kit_item`

Update the kit basic information and kit components, only support adding kit variations and updating existing kit variation’s image, price, and model_sku, don’t support deleting existing kit variations and updating the items, main component and quantity per kit of existing kit variations.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_kit_item` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/update_kit_item` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/update_kit_item` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_kit_item` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| item_id | int64 | ✅ | 28001430 | ID of kit item. |
| item_setting | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_name | string |  | item name sample | The name of this kit item. |
| ↳ images | object |  |  | Item images with 1:1 ratio. |
| ↳ ↳ image_id_list | string[] | ✅ |  | ID of image. |
| ↳ long_images | object |  |  | Item images with 3:4 ratio. |
| ↳ ↳ image_id_list | string[] | ✅ |  | ID of image. |
| ↳ video_upload_id | string[] |  | ["sg_f4bde9bc-ff3c-485e-a6dd-3161dab4b942_000000"] | Video upload ID returned from video uploading API. Only accept one video_upload_id. |
| ↳ description | string |  | item description sample | If description_type is normal, description information should be set by this field. |
| ↳ description_info | object |  |  | Rich text description field. Only whitelist sellers can use it. If you use the field, please upload the description_type=extended otherwise api will return error. If you don't use this field, you don't need to upload the description_type or upload description_type=normal |
| ↳ ↳ extended_description | object | ✅ |  | If description_type is extended , Description information should be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ field_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Field of extended description. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ field_type | string | ✅ |  | Type of extended description field. See Data Definition- description_field_type (text , image). |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ text | string | ✅ |  | If field_type is text, text information will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_info | object |  |  | If field_type is image, image will be set by this field. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_id | string | ✅ |  | Image id. |
| ↳ description_type | string |  | extended | See Data Definition- description_type (normal , extended). If you want to use extended_description, this field must be inputed. |
| ↳ logistic_info | object[] |  |  | Logistic channel setting. |
| ↳ ↳ logistic_id | int64 | ✅ |  | ID of the channel. |
| ↳ ↳ enabled | boolean | ✅ |  | Whether channel is enabled for this kit item. |
| ↳ ↳ shipping_fee | float |  |  | Shipping fee. Only needed when logistics fee_type = CUSTOM_PRICE. |
| ↳ ↳ size_id | int64 |  |  | Size ID. Only needed when logistic fee_type = SIZE_SELECTION. |
| ↳ ↳ is_free | boolean |  |  | Whether cover shipping fee for buyer. |
| ↳ unlisted | boolean |  | false | Unlist or not. |
| ↳ item_sku | string |  | item sku sample | SKU tag of item |
| ↳ weight | float |  | 1.1 | The weight of this kit item, the unit is KG. |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this kit item. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this kit item, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | Pre order setting. |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | false | Whether kit item is pre order. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int64 |  | 10 | The guaranteed days to ship orders. Please get the days_to_ship range from get_kit_item_limit api. |
| ↳ model_list | object[] |  |  | Model info list, model number at most 9. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  | 1002066586922 | ID of this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ tier_index | int64[] | ✅ | [2] | Tier index of this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ model_sku | string |  | model sku sample | Seller SKU of this kit model, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. |
| ↳ ↳ original_price | float |  | 38.3 | Original price of this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ component_list | object[] |  |  | Please get the amount of item/model that one kit model support from get_kit_item_limit. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ component_item_id | int64 | ✅ | 892568916 | ID of the item that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ component_model_id | int64 |  | 8501462426 | ID of the model that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ quantity | int64 | ✅ | 2 | The amount of the item/model that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ main_component | boolean |  | false | Whether this item/model is the main component for this kit.One kit item can only have one item/model as main component. |
| ↳ tier_variation_list | object[] |  |  | Tier variation info list.&nbsp;Only support one tier variation, and each kit item can have from 1 to 9 kit variations. |
| ↳ ↳ name | string |  |  | Tier variation name. |
| ↳ ↳ option_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Tier variation option info list. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ option | string | ✅ |  | Option name. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ image | object |  |  | Option image. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ↳ image_id | string | ✅ |  | ID of image. If you choose to define, you need to define an image for all options. |
| sync_setting | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ auto_sync_dts | boolean | ✅ |  | Auto sync the pre_order setting from main component or not. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  |  |
| message | string |  |  |  |
| warning | string |  |  |  |
| request_id | string |  |  |  |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.uat.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/update_kit_item?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "item_id": 0,
  "item_setting": {
    "description": "-",
    "description_info": {
      "extended_description": {
        "field_list": [
          {
            "field_type": "-",
            "image_info": {
              "image_id": "-"
            },
            "text": "-"
          }
        ]
      }
    },
    "description_type": "-",
    "dimension": {
      "package_height": 0.1,
      "package_length": 0.1,
      "package_width": 0.1
    },
    "images": {
      "image_id_list": [
        "-"
      ]
    },
    "item_name": "-",
    "item_sku": "-",
    "logistic_info": [
      {
        "enabled": True,
        "is_free": True,
        "logistic_id": 0,
        "shipping_fee": 0.1,
        "size_id": 0
      }
    ],
    "long_images": {
      "image_id_list": [
        "-"
      ]
    },
    "model_list": [
      {
        "component_list": [
          {
            "component_item_id": 0,
            "component_model_id": 0,
            "main_component": True,
            "quantity": 0
          }
        ],
        "model_id": 0,
        "model_sku": "-",
        "original_price": 0.1
      }
    ],
    "pre_order": {
      "days_to_ship": 0,
      "is_pre_order": True
    },
    "tier_variation_list": [
      {
        "name": "-",
        "option_list": [
          {
            "image": {
              "image_id": "-"
            },
            "option": "-"
          }
        ]
      }
    ],
    "unlisted": True,
    "video_upload_id": [
      "-"
    ],
    "weight": 0.1
  },
  "sync_setting": {
    "auto_sync_dts": True
  }
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_kit_item_info`

## v2.product.get_ssp_list

**Method:** `GET`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list`

Get the list of SSP with main info as ssp_id, product_name, gtin, oem codes.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| page_size | int32 |  | 4 | The size of one page.The limit of page_size is [1,20], and default page_size is 10. |
| offset | string |  | "" | Specifies the starting entry of data to return in the current call. Default is "", if data is more than one page, the offset can be some entry to start next call. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ page_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ has_next_page | boolean |  | true |  |
| ↳ ↳ next_offset | string |  | "10" |  |
| ↳ ssp_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ssp_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ ssp_id | int64 |  | 4567890123 | Shopee's unique identifier for Shopee&nbsp;Standard Product. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ product_name | string |  | Camiseta Azul | Name of Shopee&nbsp;Standard Product. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ gtin | string |  | 78995589784 | GTIN of Shopee&nbsp;Standard Product. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ oem | string |  | Y7ITMG8762	 | OEM of Shopee&nbsp;Standard Product. |
| ↳ ↳ available | boolean |  | true | Indicate whether the Shopee Standard Product is available to create product (One&nbsp;SSP can only be used to create one product under the shop, so if the SSP has already been used, then available will be false). |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/get_ssp_list?access_token=access_token&offset=%22%22&page_size=4&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload={}
headers = {

}
response = requests.RPCRequest("GET",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```


### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |

| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |

| ssp_id | int64 | ✅ |  | Shopee's unique identifier for Shopee&nbsp;Standard Product. |

| item_status | string |  | "UNLIST" | Item status, could be UNLIST or NORMAL. |

| ↳ enabled | boolean | ✅ | true | Whether channel is enabled for this item. |
| ↳ shipping_fee | float |  | 23.12 | Shipping fee, only needed when logistics fee_type = CUSTOM_PRICE. |
| ↳ size_id | int64 |  | 0 | Size ID, only needed when logistic fee_type = SIZE_SELECTION. |
| ↳ is_free | boolean |  | false | Whether cover shipping fee for buyer. |
| attribute_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ attribute_id | int64 | ✅ | 4990 | ID of attribute. |
| ↳ attribute_value_list | object[] | ✅ |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ value_id | int64 | ✅ | 32142 | Value ID. In the following cases, the value id needs to be uploaded as 0, and original_value_name is mandatory, needs to be filled in customized value. (1) AttributeInputType is TEXT_FILED; (2) AttributeInputType is COMBO_BOX or MULTIPLE_SELECT_COMBO_BOX, and the seller want to fill in a customized value. |
| ↳ ↳ original_value_name | string |  | Brand | Value name. original_value_name from product.get_attributes api. If value id=0, this field is required. If AttributeType is DATE_TYPE or TIMESTAMP_TYPE, you can upload timestamp(string type) as the original_value_name. |
| ↳ ↳ value_unit | string |  | kg | Unit of attribute value (quantitative attribute only). |
| pre_order | object |  |  | Pre order setting of this item. |
| ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | true | Whether this item is pre order. |
| ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  | 10 | The guaranteed days to ship orders. Please get the days_to_ship range from get_item_limit api. |
| item_sku | string |  |  | SKU tag of this item. |
| condition | string |  | "NEW" | Condition of item, could be USED or NEW. |
| wholesale | object[] |  |  | Wholesale setting of this item. |
| ↳ min_count | int32 | ✅ | 1 | Minimum count of this tier. |
| ↳ max_count | int32 | ✅ | 100 | Maximum count of this tier. |
| ↳ unit_price | float | ✅ | 28.3 | Unit price of this tier. |
| video_upload_id | string[] |  | ["sg_f4bde9bc-ff3c-485e-a6dd-3161dab4b942_000000"] | Video upload ID returned from video uploading API. Only accept one video_upload_id. |
| item_dangerous | int32 |  | 0 | This field is only applicable for&nbsp;local sellers&nbsp;in Indonesia and Malaysia. Use this field to identify whether a product is a dangerous product. 0 for non-dangerous product and 1 for dangerous product. For more information, please visit the market's respective Seller Education Hub. |
| tax_info | object |  |  | Tax information for this item. |
| ↳ ncm | string |  |  | Mercosur Common Nomenclature, it is a convention between Mercosur member countries to easily recognize goods, services and productive factors negotiated among themselves.&nbsp;(BR region)NCM must have 8 digits, OR, if your item doesn't have a NCM enter the value "00" |
| ↳ same_state_cfop | string |  |  | Tax Code of Operations and Installments for orders that seller and buyer are in the same state. It identifies a specific operation by category at the time of issuing the invoice.(BR region) |
| ↳ diff_state_cfop | string |  |  | Tax Code of Operations and Installments for orders that seller and buyer are in different states. It identifies a specific operation by category at the time of issuing the invoice.(BR region) |
| ↳ csosn | string |  |  | Code of Operation Status – Simples Nacional, code for company operations to identify the origin of the goods and the taxation regime of the operations.(BR region) |
| ↳ origin | string |  |  | Product source, domestic or foreig (BR region). |
| ↳ cest | string |  |  | Tax Replacement Specifying Code (CEST), to separate within the same NCM products that do or do not have ICMS tax substitution. (BR region)CEST must have 7 digits, OR, if your item doesn't have a CEST enter the value "00". |
| ↳ measure_unit | string |  |  | (BR region) |
| ↳ invoice_option | string |  |  | Value shuold be one of NO_INVOICES VAT_MARGIN_SCHEME_INVOICES VAT_INVOICES NON_VAT_INVOICES and if value is NON_VAT_INVOICE vat_rate should be null (PL region) |
| ↳ vat_rate | string |  |  | Value should be one of 0% 5% 8% 23% NO_VAT_RATE (PL region) |
| ↳ hs_code | string | ✅ |  | HS Code (Only for IN region) |
| ↳ tax_code | string | ✅ |  | Tax Code (Only for IN region) |
| ↳ tax_type | int32 |  |  | tax_type only for TW whitelist shop. Shopee will referred Tax type when substitute sellers for issuing e-receipts to buyers. All variations share the same tax type. The meaning of value:&nbsp;0: no tax type1: tax-able2: tax-free |
| ↳ pis | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.PIS - Programa de Integração Social (Social Integration Program). It is a government tax to collect resources for the payment of unemployment insurance and other employee related rights.PIS % - the tax applied to this product |
| ↳ cofins | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.COFINS – Contribuição para Financiamento da Seguridade Social (Contribution for Social Security Funding). It&nbsp;is a government tax to collect resources for public health system and social security.COFINS&nbsp;% - the tax applied to this product |
| ↳ icms_cst | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.ICMS - Imposto sobre Circulação de Mercadorias e Serviços (Circulation of Goods and Services Tax).&nbsp;CST - Código da Situação Tributária (Tax Situation Code) is represented by a combination of 3 numbers with the purpose of demonstrating the origin of a product and determining the form of taxation that will apply to it. Therefore, each digit in the CST Table has a specific meaning: the first digit indicates the origin of the operation, the second digit represents the ICMS taxation on the operation and the third digit provides additional information about the form of taxation. |
| ↳ pis_cofins_cst | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.The CST PIS/Cofins is a code on the Electronic Invoice (NF-e) that identifies the tax situation of PIS (Programa de Integração Social) and Cofins (Contribuição para o Financiamento da Seguridade Social) in sales of goods. |
| ↳ federal_state_taxes | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.Enter the total percentage of the combination of federal, state, and municipal taxes, using up to two decimals. |
| ↳ operation_type | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.1: Retailer2: Manufacturer |
| ↳ ex_tipi | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.The EXTIPI field in the NF-e (Nota Fiscal Eletrônica) is used to indicate if there's an exception to the IPI (Imposto sobre Produtos Industrializados) tax rate for a specific product. |
| ↳ fci_num | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.The FCI Control Number is a unique identifier assigned to each import FCI (Import Content Form). It's mandatory on the corresponding NF-e (electronic invoice) to ensure compliance with Brazilian import tax regulations. |
| ↳ recopi_num | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.RECOPI NACIONAL is a Brazilian government system that facilitates the registration and management of tax-exempt operations involving paper destined for printing books, newspapers, and periodicals (known as "papel imune" in Portuguese). |
| ↳ additional_info | string |  |  | Only for BR shop.Include relevant information to display on Invoice. |
| ↳ group_item_info | object |  |  | Only for BR shop.Required if the item is a group item. |
| ↳ ↳ group_qtd | string |  |  | Example: The package contains 6 soda cans. Whether you are selling a pack of 6 cans (fardo) or a single can (unit), enter 6. |
| ↳ ↳ group_unit | string |  |  | Example: The package contains 6 soda cans. Whether you are selling a pack of 6 cans (fardo) or a single can (unit), enter UNI for the individual can. |
| ↳ ↳ group_unit_value | string |  |  | Example: The package contains 6 soda cans. Whether you are selling a pack of 6 cans (fardo) or a single can (unity), enter the value of the individual can. |
| ↳ ↳ original_group_price | string |  |  | Example: The item is a package that contains 6 soda cans. Enter the price of the whole package. |
| ↳ ↳ group_gtin_sscc | string |  |  | Example: The item is a package that contains 6 soda cans. Please inform the GTIN SSCC code for the package. |
| ↳ ↳ group_grai_gtin_sscc | string |  |  | Example: The item is box, that contain 6 packages. Each package contains 6 soda cans. Please inform the GRAI GTIN SSCC code for the Box. |
| seller_stock | object[] |  |  | seller stock（Please notice that stock(including Seller Stock and Shopee Stock) should be larger than or equal to real-time reserved stock） |
| ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id |
| ↳ stock | int32 | ✅ |  | stock |
| size_chart_info | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ size_chart | string |  |  | ID of size chart image. If you want to remove the image size chart of the item, please pass the "size_chart" empty.Notes: Both CB shops and local shops are supported to set "size_chart". |
| ↳ size_chart_id | int64 |  |  | ID of template size chart. If you want to remove the template size chart of the item, please pass the "size_chart_id" as 0.Notes: Only local shops are supported to set "size_chart_id", for CB shops please use "size_chart". |
| authorised_brand_id | int64 |  |  | ID of authorised reseller brand. |
| model_list | object[] |  |  | Model info list. |
| ↳ tier_index | int32[] | ✅ | [0] | Tier index of this model. |
| ↳ model_sku | string |  | SKU | Seller SKU of this model, model_sku length information needs to be no more than 100 characters. |
| ↳ original_price | float | ✅ | 38.3 | Original price of this model. |
| ↳ seller_stock | object[] |  |  | seller stock（Please notice that stock(including Seller Stock and Shopee Stock) should be larger than or equal to real-time reserved stock） |
| ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | location id |
| ↳ ↳ stock | int32 | ✅ |  | stock |
| ↳ dimension | object |  |  | The dimension of this model.If don't set the dimension of this model, will use the dimension of item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ package_length | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The length of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_width | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The width of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ ↳ package_height | int32 | ✅ | 11 | The height of package for this model, the unit is CM. |
| ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | Pre-order information of this model.Notes:&nbsp;If don't set the DTS of this model, will use the DTS of the item by default. |
| ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ | true | Whether the model is pre order. |
| ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  | 10 | Days to ship. Please get the days_to_ship range from the get_item_limit API. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  | cc807adb6f8abaeb597c7b6e53e14241 | The identifier for an API request for error tracking |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ item_id | int64 |  | 892571850 | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
{
    "ssp_id": 556054528,
    "model_list": [
        {
            "tier_index": [
                0
            ],
            "original_price": 99.99,
            "seller_stock": [
                {
                    "stock": 100
                }
            ]
        },
        {
            "tier_index": [
                1
            ],
            "original_price": 119.99,
            "seller_stock": [
                {
                    "stock": 200
                }
            ]
        }
    ],
    "logistic_info": [
        {
            "logistic_id": 90003,
            "enabled": true
        }
    ]
}
```

### Response Example

```json
{
    "error": "",
    "message": "",
    "request_id": "cc807adb6f8abaeb597c7b6e53e14241",
    "response": {
        "item_id": 892571850
    },
    "warning": ""
}
```

### Error Example

```json
{
    "error": "product.error_param",
    "message": "invalid AddSSPItemRequest.SspId: SspId is required",
    "request_id": "e87af9c08c14f4d29e42915385f3e541",
    "warning": ""
}
```

### Error Codes

| Error | Description |
| --- | --- |
| `error_data` | parse data failed |
| `error_data` | data not exist |
| `error_param` | parameter invalid |
| `error_param` | The information you queried is not found. |
| `error_param` | Wrong parameters, detail: {msg}. |
| `error_server` | Something wrong. Please try later.	 |
| `error_shop` | shopid is invalid |
| `error_param` | request not from gateway |

### API Permissions

APP types that can call this API:

- ERP System
- Seller In House System
- Product Management
- Swam ERP

### Update Log

| Date | Details |
| --- | --- |
| 2024-10-25 | New API |

## v2.product.link_ssp

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/link_ssp`

Link the existing product to SSP, replacing its info for the ones from the SSP.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/link_ssp` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/link_ssp` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/link_ssp` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/link_ssp` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |

| ↳ attribute_value_list | object[] |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ value_id | int64 | ✅ | 38173 | ID of attribute value. In the following cases, the value id needs to be uploaded as 0, and original_value_name is mandatory, needs to be filled in customized value. (1) AttributeInputType is TEXT_FILED; (2) AttributeInputType is COMBO_BOX or MULTIPLE_SELECT_COMBO_BOX, and the seller want to fill in a customized value. |

If you use the field, please upload the description_type=extended otherwise api will return error. If you don't use this field, you don't need to upload the description_type or upload description_type=normal |

| ↳ ↳ is_mandatory | boolean |  | true | if this type of certification is mandatory for product |
| ↳ ↳ permit_id | int32 |  |  |  |
| ↳ ↳ name | string |  |  | Permit Type Name |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |

**Method:** `POST`

| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |

| publish_item | object | ✅ |  | Configuration details for publishing the product to the outlet shop, including model mapping, pricing, stock, logistics, and purchase limits. |
| ↳ outlet_item_id | int64 |  |  |  |

| ↳ ↳ model_status | string |  |  | The status of model. |
| ↳ ↳ original_price | float |  |  | The original price of the model. |
| ↳ ↳ seller_stock | object[] |  |  | Stock information for the model, set in outlet sku level. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ location_id | string |  |  | The location ID where the stock is stored.   |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ stock | int32 | ✅ |  | The available stock quantity for the model.   |
| ↳ ↳ pre_order | object |  |  | set in outlet sku level |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ is_pre_order | boolean | ✅ |  | Indicates whether the model is sold as a pre-order item. |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ days_to_ship | int32 |  |  | The number of days required to ship the item after an order is placed. |
| ↳ logistic_info | object[] |  |  | Logistic channel setting; can set for each outlet shop. |
| ↳ ↳ logistic_id | int64 | ✅ |  | The logistics channel ID used for shipping the item.   |
| ↳ ↳ enabled | boolean | ✅ |  | Indicates whether the logistics channel is enabled for the item.   |
| ↳ ↳ shipping_fee | float |  |  | The shipping fee charged to the buyer for this logistics channel.   |
| ↳ ↳ size_id | int64 |  |  | The parcel size ID used to calculate shipping fees.&nbsp;   |
| ↳ ↳ is_free | boolean |  |  | Indicates whether free shipping is applied for this logistics channel. |
| ↳ purchase_limit_info | object |  |  | Purchase quantity limits applied to the item in the outlet shop. |
| ↳ ↳ min_purchase_limit | int32 | ✅ |  | The minimum quantity that a buyer is allowed to purchase per order.   |
| ↳ ↳ max_purchase_limit | object | ✅ |  | The maximum purchase quantity configuration for the item.   |
| ↳ ↳ ↳ purchase_limit | int32 | ✅ |  | The maximum quantity that a buyer is allowed to purchase per order. |

| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  |  |

| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/search_unpackaged_model_list` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| page_size | int32 | ✅ | 2 | Each result set is returned as a page of entries. Use the "page_size" filters to control the maximum number of entries to retrieve per page (i.e., per call). This integer value is used to specify the maximum number of entries to return in a single "page" of data. The limit of page_size if between 1 and 48. |
| cursor | string |  |  | Specifies the starting entry of data to return in the current call. Default is "". If data is more than one page, the cursor can be some entry to start next call. |
| item_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |
| item_name | string |  | JIT | Name of the item. |
| model_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for a model under item. |
| unpackaged_sku_id | string |  |  | Unpackaged SKU ID of the model. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| warning | string |  |  | Indicate waring details if hit waring. Empty if no waring happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking. |
| response | object |  |  | Detail informations you are querying. |
| ↳ total_count | int32 |  |  | Total number of models that match the condition. |
| ↳ next_cursor | string |  |  | Pass the next_cursor in the next request as cursor to get the next page data. |
| ↳ model_list | object[] |  |  | List of models that match the condition. |
| ↳ ↳ item_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for an item. |
| ↳ ↳ item_name | string |  |  | Name of the item. |
| ↳ ↳ model_id | int64 |  |  | Shopee's unique identifier for a model under item.&nbsp;0 for no model item. |
| ↳ ↳ unpackaged_sku_id | string |  |  | Unpackaged SKU ID of the model. |


### Request Example (Python)

```python
import requests
import json

url = "https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/search_unpackaged_model_list?access_token=access_token&partner_id=partner_id&shop_id=shop_id&sign=sign&timestamp=timestamp"

payload=json.dumps({
  "page_size": 2,
  "item_name": "JIT"
})
headers = {
  'Content-Type': 'application/json'
}
response = requests.RPCRequest("POST",url,headers=headers, data=payload, allow_redirects=False)

print(response.text)

```

```json
{
	"page_size": 2,
	"item_name": "JIT"
}
```

## v2.product.generate_kit_image

**Method:** `POST`  
**Path:** `/api/v2/product/generate_kit_image`

This API generates a single consolidated image by combining the cover images of all selected items. It is typically used to create a unified product display image for kits or bundles.

### Endpoints

| Environment | URL |
| --- | --- |
| Production | `https://partner.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/generate_kit_image` |
| Chinese Mainland | `https://openplatform.shopee.cn/api/v2/product/generate_kit_image` |
| Brazil | `https://openplatform.shopee.com.br/api/v2/product/generate_kit_image` |
| Test/Sandbox | `https://partner.test-stable.shopeemobile.com/api/v2/product/generate_kit_image` |

### Common Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| partner_id | int |  | 1 | Partner ID is assigned upon registration is successful. Required for all requests. |
| timestamp | timestamp |  | 1610000000 | This is to indicate the timestamp of the request. Required for all requests. Expires in 5 minutes. |
| access_token | string |  | c09222e3fc40ffb25fc947f738b1abf1 | The token for API access, using to identify your permission to the api. Valid for multiple use and expires in 4 hours. |
| shop_id | int |  | 600000 | Shopee's unique identifier for a shop. Required param for most APIs. |
| sign | string |  | e318d3e932719916a9f9ebb57e2011961bd47abfa54a36e040d050d8931596e2 | Signature generated by partner_id, api path, timestamp, access_token, shop_id and partner_key via HMAC-SHA256 hashing algorithm. More details:  https://open.shopee.com/documents?module=87&type=2&id=58&version=2 |


### Request Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| component_list | object[] | ✅ |  | Please send up until 9 components. |
| ↳ component_item_id | int64 | ✅ |  | ID of the item that composes this kit model. |
| ↳ component_model_id | int64 |  |  | ID of the model that composes this kit model. |


### Response Parameters

| Field | Type | Required | Sample | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| error | string |  |  | Indicate error type if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| message | string |  |  | Indicate error details if hit error. Empty if no error happened. |
| request_id | string |  |  | The identifier for an API request for error tracking.&lt;path&gt;&lt;/path&gt;&lt;path&gt;&lt;/path&gt; |
| response | object |  |  |  |
| ↳ kit_image | string |  |  | generated kit image |
